{'role': 'toolResult', 'toolCallId': 'chatcmpl-tool-8a4984a085e06fb2', 'toolName': 'web_search', 'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': 'The issues you are encountering with `eraseOnClick`, `eraseOnMovablePieceClick`, and the `drawClear` functionality in Chessground relate to long-standing behavior and recent inconsistencies reported by users regarding how board markings (arrows and circles) are cleared.\n\n### Understanding the Technical Conflict\nThe core of the issue is that `drawClear` is an internal function that is often triggered by default behaviors in the library, sometimes overriding the intent of the `eraseOnClick` configuration.\n\n*   **`eraseOnClick` Limitation:** Users have noted that even when `eraseOnClick` is configured, the `drawClear` function may still execute if a user clicks on an empty square or a piece that does not match the current turn color. In the source code (specifically `drag.ts`), the condition for clearing shapes is often tied to the board state (`piece.color !== s.turnColor`) rather than being strictly gated by the `eraseOnClick` flag.\n*   **Lack of Exposure:** `drawClear` is not currently exposed in the public API, which prevents developers from easily overriding or modifying its behavior to suit specific use cases.\n\n### Recent Reported Inconsistencies\nAs of late 2025, there have been reports of "inconsistent" shape clearing behavior. Users have observed that:\n*   Markings (arrows/circles) sometimes fail to disappear immediately upon clicking, or require multiple clicks to clear.\n*   The behavior appears to change depending on whether a game is ongoing, whose turn it is, or if the board is in a specific state (e.g., against an engine).\n*   These changes have been disruptive for players accustomed to specific workflows, particularly in fast-paced variants like bullet chess, where accidental or persistent markings create distractions.\n\n### Potential Workarounds and Behavior Notes\nWhile a formal fix for the configuration overrides is pending in the repository, users and contributors have identified a few ways the current system handles clearing:\n*   **Individual Clearing:** You can generally erase a specific shape by "redrawing" it (e.g., performing the same right-click action that created it).\n*   **Manual Clear:** It has been suggested that clicking on one of your own pieces can sometimes act as a way to clear markings, though this is dependent on the specific implementation of your `movable` events.\n*   **Dragging:** Some users have found that dragging the mouse at least 1px while clicking can sometimes bypass certain "click-to-clear" triggers that are otherwise causing issues.\n\nIf you are a developer integrating Chessground, you are currently limited to managing these behaviors via the `drawable` configuration object. Since `drawClear` is internal, you may need to track the state of your shapes manually using `setShapes` or `setAutoShapes` if you require strict control over when drawings are removed.\n\n##
